# LEDGER: one row per source entry (port run 2026-11-12)

The ONLY place in calcbench where the source paths appear (D-P2). Source root (read-only):
`/Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/` — `knowledgebase/calculators/<category>/`, `knowledgebase/series/`,
`knowledgebase/ranges/`, `calculators/<vital>/`. `pending/` is triaged in [PENDING_TRIAGE.md](PENDING_TRIAGE.md), not here.

Columns: entry id | source path:line | calcbench ID and file:line | outcome | citation correction | complexity old → new | parity result | consumer change

Lead amendments: LIF-FIT-004/005 (mayhew_1rm) withdrawn before release, outcome NOT PORTED (D-P10). MED-CARDIO-009 withdrawn before release (D-P7). Sections are the builders' own words otherwise.

## Ledger W1-B1 (port_advomedix_2026-11-12)

Builder W1-B1. Source root: /Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/ (read-only).

every number below was quoted from the primary (abstract, open table, or official chart). Tests were written by the builder and run once by the reviewer (all green).

## For the lead

doc.go lines for med/scales (existing package; not edited by this builder), after MED-SCALE-006:

```
//	MED-SCALE-007  HARKScore                  HARK score (0–4): one point per yes to the four HARK questions
//	MED-SCALE-008  DangerAssessment5Count     count (0–5) of yes answers to the five DA-5 questions
```
and in the package comment: "the HARK intimate-partner-violence score (Sohal 2007) and the DA-5 count (Snider 2009)".

New packages (doc.go written): unit/temperature (TEMP), med/critical (CRIT), med/rheumatology (RHEUM),
med/hematology (HEMAT), med/infectious (INFECT), med/emergency (EMERG), med/pediatrics (PEDS), med/psychiatry (PSYCH).
Not created (no function landed): med/oncology, med/thermoregulation.

Source defects for Devin (FOUND, NOT FIXED in the reference):
- news2.go:36-54 SpO2 Scale 2 ignores air vs oxygen (≥93% on air scored 1–3; RCP Chart 1 scores it 0).
- centor_score.go computes McIsaac (age-adjusted) under a Centor 1981 citation.
- pediatric_sleep_calculator.go:18-19 returns 14–17 h under 4 months; AASM 2016 excludes that age.
- temp_liebermeister.go:19 uses 10 bpm/°C; neither Liebermeister (~8/°C) nor Cunha (10/°F) as quoted; primary not reached.
- cdiva cites the adult A-DIVA paper; smast/smast_g/dast_10/revised_trauma_score/nyha/ace_score cite a different instrument than the one computed.
- kocher_criteria and scorten JSON inputs differ from the primary's definitions (see rows).

Second-pass candidates (primary open in PMC, table not transcribed this pass): crs_grading, face_drops, flipi24, four_peps,
hiv_rasp, saps3_score, nirudak_score, duval_cibmtr (donor points need a verbatim read), wat1 (per-item points), brief_addiction_monitor.

## doc.go lines for the lead (existing packages; not edited by W1-B2)

med/hepatic/doc.go, Functions:
	MED-HEPAT-005  BARDScore  BARD score (0–4) for NAFLD from BMI, AST/ALT ratio and diabetes, Harrison 2008

med/anthropometry/doc.go, Functions (and add "Harris–Benedict basal metabolic rate, relative fat mass and the 'MUST' score and risk" to the package sentence):
	MED-ANTHRO-010  BasalMetabolicRateHarrisBenedict  predicted basal heat production (kcal/24 h), Harris & Benedict 1918
	MED-ANTHRO-011  RelativeFatMass                   relative fat mass (% body fat) from height/waist and sex, Woolcott 2018
	MED-ANTHRO-012  MUSTScore                         'MUST' total (0–6) from BMI, unplanned weight loss % and acute disease effect
	MED-ANTHRO-013  MUSTRiskCategory                  'MUST' overall risk of malnutrition, the tool's own label

## Ledger W1-B3 (cardiac, 139 entries) — med/cardio

Notes:
- Research of primaries went through WebFetch (a summariser); every ported table is consistent with the paper's
  own stated range or total where one is printed (CSRS failed that check and was withdrawn as MED-CARDIO-009).
  A reviewer reading the PDFs of the full-text-only tables (Tisdale, LACE, ATRIA stroke, ORBIT, DAPT, IE, DLCN,
  IMPEDE, TRiP(cast), Marburg, Shanghai) is recommended.
- Helpers added (unexported, in new files): `errScore` (med/cardio/score_range_error.go), `checkPositivePair`
  (cardiac_power_output.go), `checkSTEMIInputs` and `maxFinite` (subtle_anterior_stemi.go). Test helpers
  `sinkInt`, `sinkStr`, `checkErr` in timi_score_ua_nstemi_test.go.
- MOVED TO _delete/ (this builder's own superseded scratch files, never released): med/cardio/heart_rate_formulas.go,
  hemodynamic_indices.go, hemodynamic_ratio_test.go, canadian_syncope_risk_score.go, canadian_syncope_risk_score_test.go.
- IMPEDE VTE and TRiP(cast) are haematology/trauma subjects placed in med/cardio per the brief's suggested home;
  the lead may route them to med/hematology later (new IDs there; these stay as released or are renumbered before
  release, lead's call).

## Ledger W2-B6 (port_advomedix_2026-11-12)

Builder W2-B6. Source root: /Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/ (read-only); the logic of
non-runnable entries was read from each JSON's source_file under advomedix_mobile_v2/features/cross_vital_calculators/.
79 entries (renal 27, sepsis 23, trauma 29). Primaries read through PubMed efetch abstracts and Europe PMC fullTextXML
(open-access tables); every number below was quoted from the primary. Non-open-access full texts (Europe PMC
fullTextXML returns HTTP 500) were not read. Tests were written by the builder and run once by the reviewer (all green). "source case" = the app compute (no
source _test.go except ruis_incontinence); the JSONs carry no documented example.

| entry id | source path:line | calcbench ID and file:line | REUSED / PORTED / NOT PORTED / REQUESTED | citation correction | complexity old → new | parity result | consumer change |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Output is a score, not an amount, so not held under D-M5 | O(1) → O(1), no alloc | app sums 0/1 items; same score with measured SBP/HR | sbpLow/hrHigh 0/1 → systolicMmHg, heartRatePerMin values |

## Summary
- PORTED 26 entries → 27 functions: MED-TRAUMA-001…014 (med/trauma/*.go, new package), MED-CRIT-004…011 (med/critical), MED-RENAL-012…016 (med/renal).
- REUSED 6: cockcroft_gault → MED-RENAL-005/006, fe_urea → 010, fena → 009, revised_schwartz_gfr → 007/008, news → MED-CRIT-001, qsofa_calc → MED-CRIT-008.
- NOT PORTED 47: 4 dose policy (D-M5), 2 held (medicine.md §5), 5 decision pathway, 36 primary not seen (formula/table not in abstract, full text not open).
- Citation corrections: nts (wrong PMID), qsofa/qsofa_calc (website/secondary), mews/mews_calc (secondary), nexus_cspine (methodology paper), rochester_criteria (Jaskiewicz 1994), ottawa_knee_rule (issue number).
- App deviations from the primary (tests assert the primary): nts, tash_score, stone_score, pecarn_febrile_infant, shapiro_rule, ottawa_ankle_rule (1993 refined vs 1992 derived).
- Numeric choice for the lead (ASSUMPTIONS): AKINStage compares creatinine at 0.001 mg/dL (rounded thousandths) so decimal inputs compare as written.

## Ledger W2-B7 (port_advomedix_2026-11-12)

Builder W2-B7. 17 entries: nutrition 8 (runnable), obstetric 8, general/breast_milk_storage.
Source root (read-only): /Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/knowledgebase/calculators/.
Paths below are relative to it. Primaries read 2026-11-12 through PubMed eutils, Europe PMC, nationalacademies.org
"read" pages and the eCFR renderer API, via a summarising fetch tool that quoted the passages. The DGA 2020–2025 PDF
(>10 MB) could not be opened by any available tool (fetch size limit; Chrome PDF viewer not readable); curl was not
permitted. Tests written by the builder; run once by the reviewer (all green).

## Notes for the lead
- Decision needed: PercentDailyValue tie rule. 21 CFR 101.9(d)(7)(ii) gives "nearest whole percent" with no tie rule;
  implemented half-even (numeric.md default). Alternative: half-up (the source app's Math.round), which labels in
  practice commonly use. Test `TestPercentDailyValue_parity` case 3 asserts 12 (source 13).
- Mifflin REE unit: the abstract prints no unit for REE; kcal/day is documented from magnitude. Confirm from full text
  when available.
- INDEX.md rows needed for LIF-NUTR-001…007 and MED-OB-001 (new packages; doc.go written by this builder).
- doc/taxonomy.md: life/nutrition (NUTR) and med/obstetric (OB) are named in PORT_CONVENTIONS §3.

## Ledger W2-B8 (fitness, 17 entries) — life/fitness

Source root (read-only): `/Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/knowledgebase/calculators/`.
Paths below are relative to it (`fitness/<id>.json:<line of "citation">`, or the `.go` formula line).
Outcome words: REUSED / PORTED / NOT PORTED / REQUESTED. "Seen" = read in the primary (PubMed abstract, open full text, official PDF).
Units: a load whose formula is a ratio or a product is taken as `...KgOrLb` and returned in the same unit (the FENa
"one unit, any" precedent, doc/medicine_2026-11-12/spec_renal_chemistry_hepatic.md:50); the source is lb-only and refuses kg.
Kg/Lb twins lost: the equations are homogeneous of degree 1 in the load, so twins would be identical code. Decision for the lead.
Parity tests are not run by the builder (PORT_CONVENTIONS §4); "written" = cases copied as data, the reviewer runs them.

REQUESTED (num/series owner): AverageRealVariability Σ|xᵢ₊₁ − xᵢ|/(n − 1), O(n) time, O(1) space; Mena L et al.
J Hypertens 2005;23(3):505-511, PMID 15716690 — the abstract names ARV but prints no formula; read the full text first.

Assumptions used: PeakFlowZoneNHLBI reads the plan's whole-percent bands as ≥ 80 / ≥ 50 (a value between 79 and 80
takes YELLOW ZONE), the same reading as A-M3 for KDIGO. HypothermiaStageSwiss returns inTable = false above 35 °C
(Table 1 has no row there) and never returns stage IV (Table 1 gives its temperature as "Variable").
Titles confirmed by PubMed esummary (PMIDs 10903232, 27633781).

## Ledger W3-B12 (blood-sugar ranges and classifier) — med/endocrine

Source roots (read-only): `/Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/knowledgebase/ranges/vitals/blood_sugar/`
(range JSONs) and `/Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/calculators/blood_sugar/` (pipeline).
Paths below are relative to those roots. Outcome words: REUSED / PORTED / NOT PORTED / REQUESTED. "Seen" = read in the primary.
No source `_test.go` exists for these files; parity cases are the JSON band edges and the classifier's header table,
copied as data (`// parity: ledger <entry id>, source case N`). Tests not run by the builder (PORT_CONVENTIONS §4).

Primaries seen (2026-11-12):
- ADA-S2: ADA Professional Practice Committee. 2. Diagnosis and Classification of Diabetes: Standards of Care in Diabetes—2026.
  Diabetes Care 2026;49(Suppl 1):S27-S49, doi:10.2337/dc26-s002, PMID 41358893, PMC12690183. Table 2.1 "Criteria for the diagnosis
  of diabetes in nonpregnant individuals" (A1C ≥6.5% (≥48 mmol/mol); FPG ≥126 mg/dL (≥7.0 mmol/L); 2-h PG ≥200 mg/dL (≥11.1 mmol/L)
  during OGTT; random PG ≥200 mg/dL (≥11.1 mmol/L) with classic symptoms) and Table 2.2 "Criteria defining prediabetes in nonpregnant
  individuals" (A1C 5.7–6.4% (39–47 mmol/mol); FPG 100 (5.6) to 125 mg/dL (6.9) (IFG); 2-h PG 140 (7.8) to 199 mg/dL (11.0) (IGT)).
  Text: the tables "apply to children, adolescents, and adults".
- ADA-S6: ADA PPC. 6. Glycemic Goals and Hypoglycemia: Standards of Care in Diabetes—2025. Diabetes Care 2025;48(Suppl 1):S128-S145,
  doi:10.2337/dc25-s006, PMID 39651981, PMC11635034. Table 6.4 "Classification of hypoglycemia" (Level 1 <70 and ≥54 mg/dL
  (<3.9, ≥3.0 mmol/L); Level 2 <54 mg/dL (<3.0 mmol/L); Level 3 event, no glucose value). Table 6.2 repeats the CGM goals.
- Battelino 2019, PMC6973648: Table 3 (type 1/type 2: TIR >70%, TBR<70 <4%, TBR<54 <1%, TAR>180 <25%, TAR>250 <5%), Table 2
  ("Glycemic variability (%CV) target ≤36%").
- IADPSG 2010, PMC2827530 (already MED-ENDO-005/006).

## Ledger W3-B9 (series kernel, 32 exported functions) — num/series, med/endocrine, life/fitness

Source root (read-only): `/Users/devinanderson/Desktop/advo_production/advomedix-v2/backendAdvo/knowledgebase/series/`.
Paths below are relative to it. Outcome words: REUSED / PORTED / NOT PORTED / REQUESTED. "Seen" = read in the primary.
Parity tests are not run by the builder (PORT_CONVENTIONS §4); "written" = cases copied as data, the reviewer runs them.
Reuse claims are checked by num/series/reuse_parity_test.go (the source's own cases through num/stats and algo/interval).
New package num/series (SHORT SERIES, D-P8). Methods (Moments.Push/Pop/Variance/SD/CV, PrefixSums.RangeSum, Series.Cut,
Window.Contains, Date.String) are covered by the row of the function that owns their type.

## Notes for the reviewer

- num/series/reuse_parity_test.go imports num/stats and algo/interval (both lower or sibling in num, no cycle); if num/stats
  is red while its subject is live, that file fails to compile and takes the package's tests with it.
- NUM-SERIES-001 compares slopes through y − t·x (FMA, one rounding); slopes tied to within that rounding may order
  differently from their divided values; oracles use 1e-12 relative (1e-9 for the collinear large-intercept case).
